## VIII.3 The fourteen attributes with no source located

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` as to the list of attributes — mandate §11 of this task. **`NOT ESTABLISHED` as to each.**

| # | **The attribute** | **Why nothing was located** |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Member rights** | **No source located sets out the rights of members of a society, of a Section 8 company, or of a trust as against each other** |
| **2** | **Founder role** | **No Indian statute located creates or defines a *"founder"* as a legal role. **The concept is architectural, not statutory — which is itself a finding, and it is why **`CF-16`** and **`SR-20`** cannot be resolved by reference to a founder's legal position** |
| **3** | **Commercial activity** | **Whether a charitable form may carry on commercial activity at all was addressed only through the tax exemption regime — **`RF-35`, `RF-36`** — and not through entity law** |
| **4** | **Social-service activity** | **No source distinguishes it from charitable activity for entity-law purposes** |
| **5** | **Employment** | **`RF-01 … RF-10` and **`RF-28`** address employment generally; **no source located addresses employment **by a not-for-profit form** as against a commercial form** |
| **6** | **Contracts** | **Contractual capacity per form was not located, save indirectly through **`RF-20`** for a society and **`RF-23`** for an LLP** |
| **7** | **Intellectual property ownership** | **No source located addresses who owns work created by volunteers, by unpaid role-holders, or by a founder for an entity that does not yet exist. **This is the gap **`SR-12`**, **`SR-13`** and **`CQ-14`, `CQ-15`** sit in** |
| **8** | **Surplus** | **Addressed for a Section 8 company only, and only as a prohibition on **dividend** — **`RF-29`**. **Not addressed for a society, a trust or an LLP** |
| **9** | **Distributions** | **As above. **The distinction between a distribution and a **cost** — which is what **`GC-01`** turns on — was not addressed by any source** |
| **10** | **Related-party transactions** | **No source located. **`ID-1` **`RP-5`** and **`CF-16`** remain without research content** |
| **11** | **Succession** | **No source located addresses succession to a founder in any of the forms. **`LDR-U19`** and **`SR-10`** remain entirely open** |
| **12** | **Dissolution** | **Located for a Section 8 company by secondary source only, and for a society by secondary source only. **Not located for a trust, an LLP or a partnership** |
| **13** | **Reporting** | **Company filing obligations were located at **`RF-16`**; **nothing was located for a society, a trust, an LLP or a partnership** |
| **14** | **Suitability to the current economic architecture** | ### **`NOT RESEARCHED — AND IT CANNOT BE. Suitability is the professional's judgement on facts, and mandate §5 forbids this instrument from selecting an entity. **The attribute is recorded as researched-to-a-negative-result: **no source can establish suitability, because suitability is not a fact about the law`** |

| # | **Attribute** | **`OPT-A` Section 8** | **`OPT-B` Society** | **`OPT-C` Public charitable trust** | **`OPT-D` Private company** | **`OPT-E` LLP · `OPT-F` Partnership · `OPT-G` Proprietorship** | **`OPT-H` Hybrid · `OPT-I` Parent plus operating · `OPT-J` Other** |

# PART IX — `A-N16`: BANKING, SIGNING AUTHORITY, AND WHAT THE EVENTUAL STRUCTURE WILL NEED

## IX.1 The rule this Part obeys

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — `ID-1` Part X; **CEA `GF-1`, `GF-2`, `T-1 … T-5`**; mandate §13 of this task: *"do not invent a bank, a signatory, a signatory count, an account or an approval hierarchy. The research should establish what the eventual structure will need, not create the structure."*

> ### **`THIS PART INVENTS NOTHING. IT NAMES NO BANK, NO PROVIDER, NO SYSTEM, NO ACCOUNT HOLDER, NO SIGNATORY AND NO SIGNATORY COUNT. WHAT IT DOES IS LOCATE THE DOCUMENTARY REQUIREMENTS THAT ANY ACCOUNT-OPENING WOULD HAVE TO SATISFY, AND RECORD THAT THE ARCHITECTURE'S OWN TWO-SIGNATORY RULE IS AN ARCHITECTURAL CONTROL AND NOT A REGULATORY ONE — WHICH IS A DISTINCTION THE ARCHITECTURE HAS NEVER HAD.`**

## IX.2 The finding

### `RF-33` — what opening an organisational bank account requires, per legal form

| Field | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **`RESEARCH ID`** | **`RF-33`** |
| **`ARCHITECTURAL ID`** | **`A-N16`; **`SR-03`; **`BLK-A-03`; **`P0-C`; **`T-1 … T-5`; **`GF-2`; **`PP-04`; **`CQ-10`, `CQ-19`; **`LD-09`; **`ID-4`** |
| **`QUESTION`** | **What does the Reserve Bank of India require before an account may be opened for an organisation, and does it prescribe a minimum number of signatories?** |
| **`LEGAL / TAX DOMAIN`** | **Banking; **anti-money-laundering; **entity law** |
| **`RESEARCH FINDING`** | **The available source is the Reserve Bank of India (Know Your Customer) Directions, 2016, issued as Master Direction **`RBI/DBR/2015-16/18, DBR.AML.BC.No.81/14.01.001/2015-16`**, made under the Prevention of Money-Laundering Act, 2002 and the Rules thereunder. **Its Part III, *"CDD Measures for Legal Entities"*, prescribes, for each form, the certified copies that must be obtained. **For a **company** — the certificate of incorporation; **the memorandum and articles of association; **the **Permanent Account Number of the company**; **and **a resolution from the Board of Directors** together with a power of attorney granted to its managers, officers or employees to transact on its behalf, with the identification documents of the persons holding that attorney. **For a **partnership firm** — the registration certificate; **the partnership deed; **and the officially valid documents of the person holding an attorney to transact on its behalf. **For a **trust** — the registration certificate; **the trust deed; **and the officially valid documents of the person holding a power of attorney. **For an **unincorporated association or a body of individuals** — a **resolution of the managing body** of the association or body; **a power of attorney granted to transact on its behalf; **the officially valid documents of the person holding that attorney; **and **such information as may be required by the regulated entity to collectively establish the legal existence of the association or body of individuals** — with an Explanation recording that **unregistered trusts and partnership firms are included under the term *unincorporated association***. **For **juridical persons not specifically covered**, expressly including **societies**, universities and local bodies such as village panchayats — a **document showing the name of the person authorised to act on behalf of the entity**; **the identification documents of the person holding an attorney; **and **such documents as may be required by the regulated entity to establish the legal existence of such an entity or juridical person**. **Part IV requires, for a legal person who is not a natural person, that the **beneficial owner or owners be identified** and that all reasonable steps be taken in terms of **Rule 9(3) of the Prevention of Money-Laundering Rules** to verify that identity. **Section 15 of the Directions requires, when dealing with an individual who is a **beneficial owner, an authorised signatory or a power of attorney holder related to any legal entity**, a certified copy of an officially valid document containing details of identity and address, one recent photograph, **the Permanent Account Number or Form No. 60** as defined in the Income-tax Rules, 1962, and such other documents pertaining to the nature of the business or financial status as the regulated entity's policy specifies; **with a proviso that where the Permanent Account Number of the authorised signatory or power of attorney holder is not submitted, a certified copy of an officially valid document must be obtained even if it does not contain an address. **The Directions also require a board-approved know-your-customer policy; **permit video-based customer identification for authorised signatories and beneficial owners of legal-entity customers; **provide for a **'Small Account'** for an individual who possesses no officially valid document, subject to stipulated monthly and annual limits on aggregate transactions and balance; **and address the operation of bank accounts and money mules. **`NO PROVISION WAS LOCATED IN THIS RESEARCH THAT PRESCRIBES A MINIMUM NUMBER OF SIGNATORIES FOR ANY FORM OF ACCOUNT.`** |
| **`PRIMARY SOURCE`** | ### **`SRC-58` — the Reserve Bank of India (Know Your Customer) Directions, 2016, Part III §§30, 31, 32, 33 and 33B, Part IV §34, §15, §4, §18, §23 and §60, quoted at length. **`TIER 1 CONTENT`. **The document was obtained from **five independent third-party hosts** — `SRC-58`, `SRC-58a`, `SRC-58b`, `SRC-58c`, `SRC-58d` — and **not from rbi.org.in in this research**. **The hosts carry versions consolidated as at different dates, including 20 April 2020 and 10 May 2021** |
| **`SOURCE DATE / EFFECTIVE DATE`** | **Directions of 2016, coming into effect on the day they were placed on the Reserve Bank's official website, and updated periodically thereafter. **`CURRENT APPLICABILITY NOT ESTABLISHED AS TO THE LATEST CONSOLIDATED VERSION` — the text located is a consolidated version updated to 2021, and the Reserve Bank has issued updates since. **This is recorded as a limitation at **`RL-04`**, and no provision is relied on that could have been removed by a later update** |
| **`RELEVANT CONDITION`** | ### **`EVERY FORM REQUIRES TWO THINGS, AND BOTH ARE ABSENT HERE: (1) a document establishing **legal existence** — a certificate of incorporation, a registration certificate, a trust deed, a partnership deed, or information sufficient collectively to establish the legal existence of an unincorporated association or body of individuals; **and (2) a **resolution** — of the Board of Directors for a company, or of the managing body for an unincorporated association — together with an identified person authorised to act, whose own identity, address, photograph and Permanent Account Number or Form No. 60 must be produced. **Part IV adds a third: **identification and verification of the beneficial owner under Rule 9(3) of the Prevention of Money-Laundering Rules** |
| **`ENTITY DEPENDENCY`** | **`YES` — the documentary set differs by form, and every set begins with a constitutive document** |
| **`FACT DEPENDENCY`** | **`YES` — whether a managing body exists, whether any person is authorised, and who the beneficial owner is** |
| **`INTERPRETATION RISK`** | **`MEDIUM`. **The Directions are clear on what must be produced. **What is not clear from them, and what this research does not resolve, is whether an **unincorporated association or body of individuals** could in principle satisfy §33 for the purposes of this architecture — a question of real consequence, because §33's Explanation expressly includes unregistered trusts and partnership firms, and because it would still require a resolution of a managing body and collective evidence of legal existence** |
| **`WHAT THE SOURCE ACTUALLY ESTABLISHES`** | ### **`THREE THINGS. **(1) That every route to an organisational account begins with **documentary evidence of legal existence** and a **governing resolution**, and that neither exists here. **(2) That the identification requirements attach to **persons** — an authorised signatory, a power of attorney holder, and a beneficial owner — each of whom must be identified with an officially valid document, a photograph, and a Permanent Account Number or Form No. 60. **(3) That **no minimum number of signatories is prescribed anywhere in the Directions located**** |
| **`WHAT THE SOURCE DOES NOT ESTABLISH`** | ### **`That an account cannot be opened by anything other than an incorporated entity — §33 expressly contemplates unincorporated associations and bodies of individuals. That the architecture's two-signatory rule is a legal requirement — **it is not; **it is an architectural control from **[S-A: §22 N16]**. **That a bank would in practice open an account for an unincorporated body engaged in these activities, which is a matter for the bank's own policy and for a professional. That the latest consolidated version of the Directions is the version located** |
| **`SEWAK SATHI CONSEQUENCE`** | ### **`CLARIFICATION REQUIRED`, AND IT CONFIRMS THE BLOCK ON PRIMARY-SOURCE GROUNDS FOR THE FIRST TIME. **`ID-1` recorded that **`T-1`** — *"is there an entity capable of holding an account?"* — and **`T-4`** — *"is there a mandate?"* — both fail. **Until now that rested on the corpus's own analysis. **This research supplies the regulatory reason: **the Directions require a constitutive document and a governing resolution, and neither exists, so **`A-N16 REMAINS BLOCKED`** is now supported by a Tier 1 regulatory source and not only by internal analysis. **Two further consequences are recorded and neither is a decision. **First, the architecture's *"two or more signatories with a continuity rule"* is an **architectural control that exceeds what the located regulation requires** — which means it is a governance choice that may be defended on its own merits, and must not be defended as a legal necessity. **Second, **§33 opens a question the architecture has never asked: **whether an unincorporated association or body of individuals could hold an account before any entity is constituted. **That question is referred to a professional at **`PQ-06`** and is **not answered here**, because answering it would be selecting a structure** |
| **`PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION REQUIRED`** | **`YES`** |
| **`CLASSIFICATION`** | ### **`SUPPORTED BY PRIMARY SOURCE` — Tier 1 content, third-party hosts, latest consolidated version not verified** |

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` as to the chain — `ID-1` Part X, **CEA `PVR-21`**. **`[PROFESSIONAL QUESTION]` as to the discipline assignments, which are research-derived.**

| Link | **What it requires** | **The source** | **Position today** | **The discipline that answers it** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **A legal person, or an association whose legal existence can be collectively established** | **`RF-33`**, Part III of the Directions | ### **`ABSENT — `FA-4`, `PP-03`, and **`SCF-01`** for the society route** | **Corporate or entity counsel** |
| **2** | **A constitutive document — certificate of incorporation, registration certificate, trust deed, partnership deed, memorandum and articles** | **`RF-33`** | ### **`ABSENT — `DIS-29` is blank`** | **Corporate or entity counsel** |
| **3** | **A Permanent Account Number in the organisation's own name** | **`RF-33`**, and **`ER-6`** of `ID-1` | ### **`ABSENT — and `ID-1` records that the whole-word string `PAN` returns zero occurrences across the entire corpus, so this requirement was never in the architecture at all`** | **Tax professional** |
| **4** | **A resolution — of the Board of Directors for a company, or of the managing body for an unincorporated association** | **`RF-33`** | ### **`ABSENT — no governing body exists; **`APPROVES`** is unassigned in thirteen of sixteen acts** | **Governance counsel, and the body itself once constituted** |
| **5** | **An identified person authorised to act, with an officially valid document, a photograph, and a Permanent Account Number or Form No. 60** | **`RF-33`**, §15 | ### **`ABSENT — no appointment record exists; **`UD-01 … UD-14`** are unassigned** | **The entity, on governance counsel's advice** |
| **6** | **Identification and verification of the beneficial owner under Rule 9(3) of the Prevention of Money-Laundering Rules** | **`RF-33`**, Part IV | ### **`ABSENT — and it raises a question the architecture has never asked: **who is the beneficial owner of a body whose ownership is an assertion? **`FA-6`, `CF-13`, `CF-21`, `OC-10`** all bear on it and none answers it** | **Banking counsel, and a tax professional** |
| **7** | **A bank account** | **`RF-33`** | ### **`ABSENT — and no bank is named here, because naming one would be inventing a provider`** | **The entity's officers** |
| **8** | **A signing mandate with continuity** | **The architecture, not the Directions — **[S-A: §22 N16]**; **`RF-33`** locates no minimum signatory requirement** | ### **`ABSENT — `T-2`, `T-3` fail; **`LDR-U19`** is `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`, so the continuity limb has no answer even in principle** | **Governance counsel** |

> ### **`EIGHT LINKS. EVERY ONE IS ABSENT. SIX OF THE EIGHT REQUIRE AN ENTITY, A DOCUMENT OR A PERSON THAT DOES NOT EXIST, AND THE SEVENTH REQUIRES ALL SIX. THE EIGHTH IS AN ARCHITECTURAL CONTROL THAT EXCEEDS WHAT THE LOCATED REGULATION REQUIRES — AND THAT IS THE ONLY LINK IN THE CHAIN THAT THIS RESEARCH CHANGED, BY SHOWING THAT IT IS A CHOICE RATHER THAN A NECESSITY.`**

THE ARCHITECTURE HAS NOT ESTABLISHED ANY OF THOSE FACTS AND THIS RESEARCH DOES NOT ESTABLISH THEM EITHER. EVERY ACTUAL KRYPTOS CONCLUSION IN THIS PART READS:`**

## X.2 The finding

### `RF-34` — personal ownership against entity ownership, and the related-party regimes

| Field | **Content** |
|---|---|
| **`RESEARCH ID`** | **`RF-34`** |
| **`ARCHITECTURAL ID`** | **`SR-01`; **`SR-12`, `SR-13`; **`SEP-1 … SEP-8`; **`CF-02`, `CF-10`, `CF-13`, `CF-16`, `CF-21`; **`GC-01` nodes 3, 5, 6, 7, 8; **`RP-5`; **`CQ-14`, `CQ-15`, `CQ-29 … CQ-32`, `CQ-35`; **`H-Q17`; **`UE-24`** |
| **`QUESTION`** | **What does Indian law generally require for founder-owned intellectual property, licensing, related-party transactions, transfer of assets, service agreements, management fees, royalties, and payments between related entities?** |
| **`LEGAL / TAX DOMAIN`** | **Company law; **trust law; **partnership law; **tax; **intellectual property** |
| **`RESEARCH FINDING`** | **This question was researched and, in the main, could not be. **Three narrow propositions were located; **the rest of the question was not researched at all, and is recorded as such rather than filled from general knowledge. **The three propositions located are: **(1) **asset ownership differs structurally by form** — in a partnership, the partners own the assets of the firm, whereas a limited liability partnership, as an independent entity, can own assets itself; **the Indian Trusts Act vests trust property in the trustee for the benefit of the beneficiary, and s.23 makes the trustee personally liable to make good a loss caused by breach of trust; **and in a registered society, property not vested in trustees is deemed vested in the governing body. **(2) **agency differs structurally by form** — under s.18 of the Partnership Act a partner is the agent of the firm for the purposes of its business, so an act by one partner binds all, whereas under the LLP Act a partner is the agent of the LLP but not of other partners, and under s.27 of that Act the LLP is not bound by an unauthorised act where the third party knew of the want of authority. **(3) **a conflict between an assertion of personal control and a recorded documentary establishment is a live issue in this architecture's own materials** — `CF-21` records **`FA-6`**, the founder's assertion that Krytos is intended to be controlled and owned by him, against a clause recording Krytos as *"the parent/company context and ownership context already established in v1.0"*, an establishment said to be documented; **and `CF-13` records a source verification that *"no invented ownership — VERIFIED"*, which remains valid because an assertion is not an instrument. **Nothing was located on: **the transfer-pricing regime; **the related-party regime for companies, societies, trusts or limited liability partnerships; **service agreements or management fees between related entities; **royalties; **licensing of a name, a mark or a work; **the ownership of intellectual property created by a volunteer, by an unpaid role-holder, or by a founder for an entity that does not yet exist; **or the tax treatment of a contribution of assets by a founder to an entity he controls** |
| **`PRIMARY SOURCE`** | **For the three located propositions: **`SRC-50`**, **`SRC-51`**, **`SRC-52`** (LLP and partnership asset ownership and agency); **`SRC-37`** — India Code, Indian Trusts Act, 1882 (**Tier 1**); **`SRC-73`** (society property). **For the unlocated propositions: **none**** |
| **`SOURCE DATE / EFFECTIVE DATE`** | **As per the underlying sources; **all `CURRENT`** |
| **`RELEVANT CONDITION`** | **Every proposition located is conditional on a form having been chosen and on the relevant relationship having been created by an instrument** |
| **`ENTITY DEPENDENCY`** | ### **`YES — wholly. Personal ownership against entity ownership is a question with no answer until there is an entity`** |
| **`FACT DEPENDENCY`** | ### **`YES — wholly. **`DIS-11`, `DIS-12`, `DIS-29`, `DIS-30` are blank, and **`OC-10`** records that **0 of 17 asset classes have a stated owner**** |
| **`INTERPRETATION RISK`** | **`HIGH` — the question is broad, the located material is narrow, and the temptation to generalise from three propositions to a related-party regime is exactly what mandate §12 forbids** |
| **`WHAT THE SOURCE ACTUALLY ESTABLISHES`** | **That asset ownership and agency are **structural consequences of the choice of form**, and not matters that can be settled by agreement after the fact; **that a trustee's personal liability for breach is primary-source established; **and that this architecture already contains a documented conflict between an assertion of ownership and a recorded establishment of ownership** |
| **`WHAT THE SOURCE DOES NOT ESTABLISH`** | ### **`Anything about Krytos. Not that it is a company, a trust, a society, a limited liability partnership, a legal person, a parent, a subsidiary, an owner of intellectual property, a data fiduciary or a contracting party. **`UE-24`** continues to block **`OPT-I`**. **`H-Q17` — *"Can Sewak Sathi pay Krytos?"* — remains `UNKNOWN`, and this research found no source bearing on it, because the answer turns on whether Krytos is a legal person and nothing located establishes that it is`** |
| **`SEWAK SATHI CONSEQUENCE`** | ### **`BLOCKED PENDING PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` for the whole of the related-party, licensing, transfer and royalty subject matter, and `CLARIFICATION REQUIRED` for the three located propositions. **The practical consequence is that **`ID-1`'s eleven questions in block F — **`CQ-28 … CQ-35`** — are almost entirely unaffected by this research, and **`GC-01`'s nodes 5, 6, 7 and 8 remain exactly where the delta audit left them: **reachable and re-framed, open, facts supplied but opinion required, and authority unresolved**. **Mandate §12 is observed: **every actual Krytos conclusion in this instrument reads `UNRESOLVED UNTIL FACTUAL OR LEGAL STATUS IS ESTABLISHED`** |
| **`PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION REQUIRED`** | **`YES`** |
| **`CLASSIFICATION`** | ### **`NOT ESTABLISHED` for the bulk of the question; **`PARTIALLY SUPPORTED`** for the three located propositions** |

## X.3 What remains unresolved, stated once and completely

**[P]** `[SOURCE]` — `ID-1` Part XII; **CEA Part XVII**; **`SEP-1 … SEP-8`**; the ten-pairing transfer matrix; **`H-Q15 … H-Q19`**.

| Item | **Position after this research** |
|---|---|
| **Is Krytos a legal person?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED UNTIL FACTUAL OR LEGAL STATUS IS ESTABLISHED` — and **`GC-01` node 1** remains closed **negatively** on **`FA-4`**, which research cannot displace** |
| **Does Krytos own anything?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED`. **`OC-10`: 0 of 17 asset classes have a stated owner. **`CF-13`: *"no invented ownership — VERIFIED"* still holds** |
| **Is Krytos a parent?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED`. **`FA-2` is an assertion. **`UE-24` blocks **`OPT-I`** because two legal persons are required and at most one can be constituted at a time** |
| **May Sewak Sathi pay Krytos?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED` — **`H-Q17`** remains `UNKNOWN`, `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION`. **`CQ-31`** remains class ⑩, *cannot yet be answered*. **No source located bears on it** |
| **May Krytos pay Sewak Sathi?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED` — **`H-Q19`** records that priority 8, *residual surplus / founder upside*, names a recipient who is not identified** |
| **Who owns the founder's work — the architecture, the curricula, the name, the systems?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED`. **No source located. **`SR-12`, `SR-13` remain `EXPLICITLY UNRESOLVED`; **`DIS-30` is blank** |
| **May the founder contribute assets, and on what terms?** | ### **`UNRESOLVED`. **No source located on the tax or company-law treatment of a founder's contribution to an entity he controls. **`AG-10` capture remains the architectural requirement** |
| **Is the founder a related party in his personal capacity?** | **`FACTS SUPPLIED AT E1 — OPINION STILL REQUIRED`, exactly as **`GC-01` node 7** recorded it. **This research added no source to it** |
| **Who holds the conflict-of-interest discipline over priorities 4 and 8?** | ### **`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED` — **`GC-01` node 8**, **`CF-16`**, **`GD-01`, `GD-08`. **No source can supply an authority, and this instrument names none** |
| **The eight separations at `SEP-1 … SEP-8`** | ### **`ALL EIGHT REMAIN ARCHITECTURAL REQUIREMENTS. **Research located no statutory mechanism that performs any of them, and **`SEP-8`** — the separation of what is architecturally desirable from what is legally permissible — is the separation this entire instrument exists to honour** |
